## Boca Beach

`osm:relation/8460864` · 26.7550914, -82.267814 · beach type: **sandy** (OpenStreetMap surface=sand) · lifeguard: no

- **`us-fl-boca-beach-1.jpg`** ⚠︎ *context, not the coast itself* — “Boca Grande Entrance Rear Range (Gasperilla Island Lighthouse) (16932303875)” by Pete Markham from Loretto, USA, Wikimedia Commons, CC BY-SA 2.0
  - Source page: https://commons.wikimedia.org/wiki/File:Boca_Grande_Entrance_Rear_Range_(Gasperilla_Island_Lighthouse)_(16932303875).jpg

  - Dated: Taken on 25 March 2015, 09:18
  - 1600×1060px, 356 KB, shot 1516 m from the spot
  - Description: This is an interesting lighthouse. This is an onshore skeletal lighthouse with a hexagonal footprint. It was originally built in 1881 as the Delaware Breakwater Range Rear. That light was discontinued in 1918. It was disassembled in 1921 and relocated to Florida. In 1927, it was moved to Gasperilla Island, FL to be a rear range light there. Oddly, the completed tower was not put into service until 1932. One other oddity about this lighthouse is the complete lack of watchroom below the lantern. Normally, a lighthouse has a small room below the lantern that would be used by the keeper during ope
  - Match: score 12 via coastal-in-title, name:boca, kw:shore+mar+break+lighthouse, geo:1516m, w>=1000, landscape
